PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object a craves object b, object d craves object c, harmony, planet object b, planet object c, province object a and province object d.
My goal is to have that object c craves object a.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================

------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - obj)
    (planet ?x - obj)
    (pain ?x - obj)
    (craves ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)

  (:objects
    a b c d - obj
  )

  (:init
    (craves a b)
    (craves d c)
    (harmony)
    (planet b)
    (planet c)
    (province a)
    (province d)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (craves c a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types obj)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - obj)
    (planet ?x - obj)
    (pain ?x - obj)
    (craves ?x - obj ?y - obj)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - obj ?y - obj)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here is the human-readable action sequence that follows the given plan exactly:

1. **Time 0:** **Feast d from c**  
   - Object **d** feasts from object **c**.  
   - This is allowed because **d craves c**, **d** is a **province**, and **harmony** holds.

2. **Time 1:** **Succumb d**  
   - Object **d** succumbs.  
   - This is allowed because **d** is in **pain** after the previous step.

3. **Time 2:** **Attack c**  
   - Object **c** is attacked.  
   - This is allowed because **c** is now both a **province** and a **planet**, and **harmony** holds.

4. **Time 3:** **Overcome c from a**  
   - Object **c** overcomes using object **a**.  
   - This is allowed because **a** is a **province** and **c** is in **pain**.  
   - After this step, **c craves a**, which achieves the goal.

**Goal reached:** object **c** craves object **a**.

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)

  (:types
    object
  )

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (harmony)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem_instance)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)

  (:objects
    a b c d - object
  )

  (:init
    (craves b c)
    (craves c d)
    (craves d a)
    (harmony)
    (planet a)
    (province b)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (craves a c)
      (craves d a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

  (:requirements :strips :typing)

  (:types
    thing
  )

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - thing)
    (planet ?x - thing)
    (pain ?x - thing)
    (craves ?x - thing ?y - thing)
    (harmony)
  )

    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)

      (harmony)
    )

      (pain ?x)

      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here is the human-readable plan, following the given PDDL plan exactly:

1. **Time 0:** **Attack object d**  
   - Preconditions satisfied: object **d** is both a **province** and a **planet**, and **harmony** holds.
   - Effect: object **d** becomes in **pain**, and it is no longer a **province** or a **planet**. **Harmony** also becomes false.

2. **Time 1:** **Overcome object d from object c**  
   - Preconditions satisfied: object **c** is a **province**, and object **d** is in **pain**.
   - Effect: **harmony** becomes true again, object **d** becomes a **province**, and **d craves c** becomes true. Object **c** is no longer a **province**, and object **d** is no longer in **pain**.

After these 2 steps, the goal is satisfied:
- **b craves a** still holds.
- **d craves c** now holds.

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types thing)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - thing)
    (planet ?x - thing)
    (pain ?x - thing)
    (craves ?x - thing ?y - thing)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

    (province d)

  )

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here is the human-readable version of the plan, following the given action sequence exactly.

1. **Time 1:** **Feast d from a**  
   - Object **d** feasts from object **a**.  
   - This uses the fact that **d craves a**, **d** is a province, and **harmony** holds.

2. **Time 2:** **Succumb d**  
   - Object **d** succumbs.  
   - This restores **d** as a province and a planet, and restores **harmony**.

3. **Time 3:** **Feast a from c**  
   - Object **a** feasts from object **c**.  
   - This uses the fact that **a craves c**, **a** is a province, and **harmony** holds.

4. **Time 4:** **Succumb a**  
   - Object **a** succumbs.  
   - This restores **a** as a province and a planet, and restores **harmony**.

5. **Time 5:** **Feast c from b**  
   - Object **c** feasts from object **b**.  
   - This uses the fact that **c craves b**, **c** is a province, and **harmony** holds.

6. **Time 6:** **Succumb c**  
   - Object **c** succumbs.  
   - This restores **c** as a province and a planet, and restores **harmony**.

7. **Time 7:** **Attack a**  
   - Object **a** is attacked.  
   - Since **a** is both a province and a planet, and **harmony** holds, this creates **pain on a**.

8. **Time 8:** **Overcome a from b**  
   - Object **a** overcomes using object **b**.  
   - Because **b** is a province and **a** is in pain, this makes **a crave b**.

9. **Time 9:** **Attack d**  
   - Object **d** is attacked.  
   - Since **d** is both a province and a planet, and **harmony** holds, this creates **pain on d**.

10. **Time 10:** **Overcome d from c**  
    - Object **d** overcomes using object **c**.  
    - Because **c** is a province and **d** is in pain, this makes **d crave c**.

**Result:**  
At the end of this sequence, the goal is satisfied:
- **a craves b**
- **d craves c**

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)

  (:objects
    a b c d - object
  )

  (:init
    (craves a d)
    (craves b c)
    (craves d b)
    (harmony)
    (planet c)
    (province a)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (craves a c)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

Here is the human-readable action sequence that follows the given plan exactly:

1. **Time 0:** **Feast `a` from `d`.**  
   `a` feasts from `d`, using the fact that `a` craves `d`, `a` is a province, and harmony holds.

2. **Time 1:** **Succumb `a`.**  
   Since `a` is in pain, `a` succumbs, restoring `a` as both a province and a planet, and restoring harmony.

3. **Time 2:** **Feast `d` from `b`.**  
   `d` feasts from `b`, using the fact that `d` craves `b`, `d` is a province, and harmony holds.

4. **Time 3:** **Succumb `d`.**  
   Since `d` is in pain, `d` succumbs, restoring `d` as both a province and a planet, and restoring harmony.

5. **Time 4:** **Feast `b` from `c`.**  
   `b` feasts from `c`, using the fact that `b` craves `c`, `b` is a province, and harmony holds.

6. **Time 5:** **Succumb `b`.**  
   Since `b` is in pain, `b` succumbs, restoring `b` as both a province and a planet, and restoring harmony.

7. **Time 6:** **Attack `a`.**  
   With `a` being both a province and a planet, and with harmony true, attack `a`, causing `a` to be in pain.

8. **Time 7:** **Overcome `a` from `c`.**  
   Since `c` is a province and `a` is in pain, overcome `a` from `c`. This restores harmony, makes `a` a province, and establishes that **`a` craves `c`**.

**Result:** The final goal is satisfied: **`a` craves `c`**.

FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types thing)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - thing)
    (planet ?x - thing)
    (pain ?x - thing)
    (craves ?x - thing ?y - thing)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)

  (:objects
    a b c d - thing
  )

  (:init
    (craves b c)
    (harmony)
    (planet a)
    (planet c)
    (planet d)
    (province a)
    (province b)
    (province d)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (craves a b)
      (craves b d)
      (craves d c)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object c craves object d, object d craves object a, harmony, planet object a, planet object b, province object b and province object c.
My goal is to have that object a craves object c, object b craves object a and object d craves object b.

My plan is as follows:

    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]

My goal is to have that object a craves object c, object b craves object a and object d craves object b.

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types object)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - object)
    (planet ?x - object)
    (pain ?x - object)
    (craves ?x - object ?y - object)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - object)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

    :parameters (?x - object)

      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - object ?y - object)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

**Initial situation:**
- c craves d
- d craves a
- harmony is true
- a is a planet
- b is a planet
- b is a province
- c is a province

**Plan execution:**

1. **t=1 — Feast c from d**  
   Since **c craves d**, **c is a province**, and **harmony** holds, c feasts from d.  
   Effects:
   - c is now in **pain**
   - d becomes a **province**
   - c no longer craves d
   - c is no longer a province
   - harmony becomes false

2. **t=2 — Succumb c**  
   Since **c is in pain**, c succumbs.  
   Effects:
   - c becomes a **province**
   - c becomes a **planet**
   - **harmony** becomes true
   - c is no longer in pain

   - d no longer craves a
   - d is no longer a province
   - harmony becomes false

FINAL-PLAN

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types thing)

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - thing)
    (planet ?x - thing)
    (pain ?x - thing)
    (craves ?x - thing ?y - thing)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

  (:objects
    a b c d - thing
  )

  (:init

    (craves d a)
    (harmony)
    (planet a)
    (planet b)
    (province c)
    (province d)
  )

  (:goal

      (craves b c)
    )
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here is the human-readable plan, following the given PDDL plan exactly.

1. At time 1, **feast c from b**.  
   - Object **c** feasts from object **b**.  
   - As a result, **c** is in pain, **b** becomes a province object, and **c** no longer craves **b**. Harmony is lost.

2. At time 2, **succumb c**.  
   - Object **c** succumbs.  
   - As a result, **c** becomes a province object and a planet object again, harmony is restored, and **c** is no longer in pain.

3. At time 3, **feast d from a**.  
   - Object **d** feasts from object **a**.  
   - As a result, **d** is in pain, **a** becomes a province object, and **d** no longer craves **a**. Harmony is lost.

4. At time 4, **succumb d**.  
   - Object **d** succumbs.  
   - As a result, **d** becomes a province object and a planet object again, harmony is restored, and **d** is no longer in pain.

5. At time 5, **attack a**.  
   - Object **a** is attacked.  
   - Since **a** is both a province object and a planet object, and harmony holds, the attack is valid.  
   - As a result, **a** is now in pain, and it is no longer a province object or a planet object. Harmony is lost.

6. At time 6, **overcome a from d**.  
   - Object **a** overcomes using object **d**.  
   - Since **d** is a province object and **a** is in pain, this action is valid.  
   - As a result, harmony is restored, **a** becomes a province object, **a craves d**, and **d** is no longer a province object. **a** is no longer in pain.

7. At time 7, **attack b**.  
   - Object **b** is attacked.  
   - Since **b** is both a province object and a planet object, and harmony holds, the attack is valid.  
   - As a result, **b** is now in pain, and it is no longer a province object or a planet object. Harmony is lost.

8. At time 8, **overcome b from c**.  
   - Object **b** overcomes using object **c**.  
   - Since **c** is a province object and **b** is in pain, this action is valid.  
   - As a result, harmony is restored, **b** becomes a province object, **b craves c**, and **c** is no longer a province object. **b** is no longer in pain.

Final goal achieved:
- **a craves d**
- **b craves c**

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)

  (:types
    thing
  )

  (:predicates
    (province ?x - thing)
    (planet ?x - thing)
    (pain ?x - thing)
    (craves ?x - thing ?y - thing)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (planet ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?x - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (province ?x)
      (planet ?x)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (province ?y)
      (pain ?x)
    )
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?x)
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (not (province ?y))
      (not (pain ?x))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?x - thing ?y - thing)
    :precondition (and
      (craves ?x ?y)
      (province ?x)
      (harmony)
    )
    :effect (and
      (pain ?x)
      (province ?y)
      (not (craves ?x ?y))
      (not (province ?x))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem jack_of_all_trades_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades_instance)

  (:objects
    a b c d - thing
  )

  (:init
    (craves a d)
    (craves b c)
    (harmony)
    (planet c)
    (planet d)
    (province a)
    (province b)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (craves b d)
      (craves d a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Here is the human-readable action sequence that follows the given plan exactly.

1. At time 0, **feast a from d**.  
   - Because **a craves d**, **a** is a **province**, and **harmony** holds, this action is allowed.  
   - After this, **a** is in **pain**, **d** becomes a **province**, and **a no longer craves d**. Harmony is lost.

2. At time 1, **succumb a**.  
   - Since **a** is in **pain**, this action is allowed.  
   - After this, **a** becomes a **province** again, **a** becomes a **planet**, and **harmony** is restored. **a** is no longer in pain.

3. At time 2, **attack d**.  
   - Since **d** is now both a **province** and a **planet**, and **harmony** holds, this action is allowed.  
   - After this, **d** is in **pain** and is no longer a **province** or a **planet**. Harmony is lost.

4. At time 3, **overcome d from a**.  
   - Since **a** is a **province** and **d** is in **pain**, this action is allowed.  
   - After this, **harmony** is restored, **d** becomes a **province**, and **d craves a**. **a** stops being a province, and **d** is no longer in pain.

5. At time 4, **feast b from c**.  
   - Because **b craves c**, **b** is a **province**, and **harmony** holds, this action is allowed.  
   - After this, **b** is in **pain**, **c** becomes a **province**, and **b no longer craves c**. Harmony is lost.

6. At time 5, **overcome b from d**.  
   - Since **d** is a **province** and **b** is in **pain**, this action is allowed.  
   - After this, **harmony** is restored, **b** becomes a **province**, and **b craves d**. **d** stops being a province, and **b** is no longer in pain.

Final result:  
- **b craves d**  
- **d craves a**  

So the goal is achieved.
